```json
  "content": "&lt;agent_context>\nRSFT pipeline 代码在 /root/autodl-tmp/freige/training/ 下：\n- rsft_generate.py: 生成脚本（正在 cuda:1 运行）\n- rsft_score_filter.py: CED 打分+筛选脚本（参考其中 CED 评分逻辑）\n- rsft_trainer.py: RSFT 训练脚本\n\nCED scoring 核心：用 NLI cross-encoder 对 (evidence_sentence, claim) 打分，衡量 evidence 对 claim 的支持度。具体实现参考 rsft_score_filter.py。\n\n生成数据格式：每个 batch 是 JSON 文件，在 /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_generations/ 目录下。每个文件包含若干 document 的 8 个候选输出。\n&lt;/agent_context>\n\n&lt;project_context>\n# 项目: faith-evidence-ie\n\n## 运行环境\n- 服务器名: rtx5090-4x\n- SSH 账户: root（调用 ssh_execute / submit_training_job 时传入 account=\"root\"）\n- SSH 连接命令: ssh rtx5090-4x  # 必须用此命令，~/.ssh/config 自动应用密钥和跳板机，禁止用裸 IP\n- 项目代码根目录: /root/autodl-tmp/freige\n\n\n## 环境搭建\n连接服务器后先调 `detect_image` 检测是否为预置镜像。如果匹配，跳过基础环境搭建，只装项目特有依赖。已预装的包不要重复安装。\n\n## 输出风格\n回复简洁直接。只输出执行结果和必要信息，不要解释过程、不要客套、不要给未被要求的建议。\n&lt;/project_context>\n## 任务：D032 RSFT Diversity 诊断\n\n### 背景\n我们在 rtx5090-4x 的 cuda:1 上跑 RSFT 生成（SFT 模型对每个训练样本生成 N=8 候选输出，T=0.7）。目前已生成约 83/1514 batches，输出在 `/root/autodl-tmp/rsft_generations/`。\n\n前期监控发现 diversity 危机：mean_diversity（unique triple-sets per 8 generations）只有 0.9/8，远低于 probe 时的 7.0/8。但我们怀疑即使 triple-set 相似，CED score（对比 evidence 质量评分）可能仍有方差——如果方差足够大，RSFT 仍然可行（因为我们用 CED score 筛选，不是用 triple diversity）。\n\n### 你的任务\n\n1. **SSH 到 rtx5090-4x** 检查 `/root/autodl-tmp/rsft_generations/` 的当前状态（多少 batch 文件、文件格式）\n\n2. **写一个诊断脚本** `/root/autodl-tmp/rsft_diversity_diag.py`，在 cuda:2 上运行，功能：\n   - 加载所有已生成的 batch 文件（JSON 格式，每个 batch 包含多个 document 的 8 个 generations）\n   - 先读一个 batch 文件看数据结构，搞清字段名\n   - 对每个 document 的 8 个 generation，用 CED scoring 计算 ced_score\n   - CED scoring 逻辑参考 `/root/autodl-tmp/freige/training/rsft_score_filter.py` 中的 CED 评分部分（使用 NLI cross-encoder 模型 `/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/cross-encoder/nli-deberta-v3-base`）\n   - 计算每个 document 的 8 个 ced_score 的 mean 和 std\n   - 计算 evidence sentence_ids 的 overlap（8 个 generation 之间的 Jaccard 相似度）\n   - 输出汇总统计：\n     * 总 document 数\n     * mean_std(ced_score)：所有 document 的 ced_score std 的平均值\n     * median_std(ced_score)\n     * top2-bottom2 gap：每个 document 中 ced_score 最高 2 个和最低 2 个的平均差\n     * mean evidence Jaccard（越低=evidence 越多样）\n     * ced_score 分布（min, p25, median, p75, max）\n\n3. **运行脚本**：`CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=2 python /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_diversity_diag.py`\n   - 用 submit_training_job 不合适（这是短时诊断任务），直接 ssh_execute 运行\n   - 如果运行超时，先跑前 20 个 batch 子集出快速结果\n\n4. **判断标准**（Director 指定）：\n   - mean_std(ced_score) > 0.05 AND top2-bottom2 gap > 0.1 → RSFT 可行，继续跑\n   - mean_std(ced_score) < 0.02 → 需要策略变更\n   - 中间值 → 报告数据，由 Director 判断\n\n5. **注意事项**：\n   - **不要动 cuda:1 上的生成进程**（PID 122144），它在继续跑\n   - cuda:0 在跑 GRPO training，cuda:3 在跑 ckpt-100 eval，都不要碰\n   - 环境激活：`source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`\n   - HF_HOME=/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache\n\n6. **输出**：最终汇报所有统计数字 + 你的可行性判断（viable / marginal / not viable）\n\n### 服务器信息\n- 服务器：rtx5090-4x（account=root）\n- Python 环境：conda base（/root/miniconda3/bin/python）\n- 项目路径：/root/autodl-tmp/freige/\n- NLI 模型路径：/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/cross-encoder/nli-deberta-v3-base (已下载好的 cross-encoder)\n"
}
```

## 任务：D032 RSFT Diversity 诊断

### 背景
我们在 rtx5090-4x 的 cuda:1 上跑 RSFT 生成（SFT 模型对每个训练样本生成 N=8 候选输出，T=0.7）。目前已生成约 83/1514 batches，输出在 `/root/autodl-tmp/rsft_generations/`。

前期监控发现 diversity 危机：mean_diversity（unique triple-sets per 8 generations）只有 0.9/8，远低于 probe 时的 7.0/8。但我们怀疑即使 triple-set 相似，CED score（对比 evidence 质量评分）可能仍有方差——如果方差足够大，RSFT 仍然可行（因为我们用 CED score 筛选，不是用 triple diversity）。

### 你的任务

1. **SSH 到 rtx5090-4x** 检查 `/root/autodl-tmp/rsft_generations/` 的当前状态（多少 batch 文件、文件格式）

2. **写一个诊断脚本** `/root/autodl-tmp/rsft_diversity_diag.py`，在 cuda:2 上运行，功能：
   - 加载所有已生成的 batch 文件（JSON 格式，每个 batch 包含多个 document 的 8 个 generations）
   - 先读一个 batch 文件看数据结构，搞清字段名
   - 对每个 document 的 8 个 generation，用 CED scoring 计算 ced_score
   - CED scoring 逻辑参考 `/root/autodl-tmp/freige/training/rsft_score_filter.py` 中的 CED 评分部分（使用 NLI cross-encoder 模型 `/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/cross-encoder/nli-deberta-v3-base`）
   - 计算每个 document 的 8 个 ced_score 的 mean 和 std
   - 计算 evidence sentence_ids 的 overlap（8 个 generation 之间的 Jaccard 相似度）
   - 输出汇总统计：
     * 总 document 数
     * mean_std(ced_score)：所有 document 的 ced_score std 的平均值
     * median_std(ced_score)
     * top2-bottom2 gap：每个 document 中 ced_score 最高 2 个和最低 2 个的平均差
     * mean evidence Jaccard（越低=evidence 越多样）
     * ced_score 分布（min, p25, median, p75, max）

3. **运行脚本**：`CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=2 python /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_diversity_diag.py`
   - 用 submit_training_job 不合适（这是短时诊断任务），直接 ssh_execute 运行
   - 如果运行超时，先跑前 20 个 batch 子集出快速结果

4. **判断标准**（Director 指定）：
   - mean_std(ced_score) > 0.05 AND top2-bottom2 gap > 0.1 → RSFT 可行，继续跑
   - mean_std(ced_score) < 0.02 → 需要策略变更
   - 中间值 → 报告数据，由 Director 判断

5. **注意事项**：
   - **不要动 cuda:1 上的生成进程**（PID 122144），它在继续跑
   - cuda:0 在跑 GRPO training，cuda:3 在跑 ckpt-100 eval，都不要碰
   - 环境激活：`source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`
   - HF_HOME=/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache

6. **输出**：最终汇报所有统计数字 + 你的可行性判断（viable / marginal / not viable）

### 服务器信息
- 服务器：rtx5090-4x（account=root）
- Python 环境：conda base（/root/miniconda3/bin/python）
- 项目路径：/root/autodl-tmp/freige/
- NLI 模型路径：/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/cross-encoder/nli-deberta-v3-base (已下载好的 cross-encoder)

```text
{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"RSFT Step 2: CED 打分 + 筛选。\\n\\n读取 rsft_generate.py 生成的 JSONL，对每个候选输出计算三种奖励:\\n  - format_reward: JSON 格式合规性\\n  - f1_reward: 与 gold triples 的 micro-F1\\n  - ced_reward: CED 对比证据判别奖励\\n\\n按组合阈值筛选高质量样本，输出 SFT 训练兼容格式。\\n\\n输入:\\n  - generations.jsonl (rsft_generate.py 的输出)\\n\\n输出 (保存到 --output_path):\\n  - rsft_train.json: 筛选后的 SFT 训练数据\\n  - rsft_scores.jsonl: 带分数的完整记录（供分析）\\n  - rsft_report.json: 统计报告\\n\\n依赖: transformers, torch, freige.rewards.ced_reward\\n\\n用法:\\n  python -m freige.training.rsft_score_filter \\\\\\n      --input_dir /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_generations \\\\\\n      --output_path /root/autodl-tmp/rsft_filtered \\\\\\n      --selection_strategy top_pct --top_pct 25 \\\\\\n      --nli_model_path /root/autodl-tmp/models/nli-deberta-v3-base\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport logging\\nimport os\\nfrom collections import defaultdict\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\n\\nlogging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO, format=\\\"%(asctime)s %(levelname)s %(message)s\\\")\\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\\n\\nSYSTEM_PROMPT = (\\n    \\\"You are an information extraction model. Given a document with numbered sentences \\\"\\n    \\\"and a list of entities, extract all relation triples. For each triple, provide the \\\"\\n    \\\"head entity, relation type, tail entity, and the sentence IDs that serve as evidence. \\\"\\n    \\\"Output as a JSON list.\\\"\\n)\\n\\n\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n# Reward functions — 复用 grpo_trainer 中的逻辑\\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\\n\\ndef compute_format_reward(parsed_triples, format_ok):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"格式奖励，复用 grpo_trainer.format_reward_fn 的逻辑。\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    if not format_ok:\\n        return 0.0\\n    if not parsed_triples:\\n        return 0.1\\n    valid = 0\\n    for item in parsed_triples:\\n        if (isinstance(item, dict) and \\\"head\\\" in item\\n                and \\\"relation\\\" in item and \\\"tail\\\" in item\\n                and \\\"evidence\\\" in item\\n                and isinstance(item[\\\"evidence\\\"], list)):\\n            valid += 1\\n    return valid / len(parsed_triples)\\n\\n\\ndef compute_f1_reward(parsed_triples, gold_triples):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"F1 奖励，复用 grpo_trainer.f1_reward_fn 的逻辑。\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    gold_set = set()\\n    for g in gold_triples:\\n        gold_set.add((\\n            str(g.get(\\\"head\\\") or \\\"\\\").lower().strip(),\\n            str(g.get(\\\"relation\\\") or \\\"\\\").lower().strip(),\\n            str(g.get(\\\"tail\\\") or \\\"\\\").lower().strip(),\\n        ))\\n\\n    pred_set = set()\\n    for p in parsed_triples:\\n        if isinstance(p, dict):\\n            pred_set.add((\\n                str(p.get(\\\"head\\\") or \\\"\\\").lower().strip(),\\n                str(p.get(\\\"relation\\\") or \\\"\\\").lower().strip(),\\n                str(p.get(\\\"tail\\\") or \\\"\\\").lower().strip(),\\n            ))\\n\\n    if not gold_set:\\n        return 1.0 if not pred_set else 0.0\\n\\n    tp = len(pred_set & gold_set)\\n    prec = tp / len(pred_set) if pred_set else 0.0\\n    rec = tp / len(gold_set) if gold_set else 0.0\\n    f1 = 2 * prec * rec / (prec + rec) if (prec + rec) > 0 else 0.0\\n    return f1\\n\\n\\ndef compute_ced_reward_for_record(record, ced_model, tau=0.5):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"CED 奖励，复用 grpo_trainer.CEDRewardWrapper.__call__ 的逻辑。\\n\\n    对一条记录中的所有 parsed_triples <details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>
```

```text
Now I have a clear picture:

1. **Data structure**: `generations.jsonl` with 1744 lines. Each line is a JSONL record with:
   - `doc_id`: document identifier
   - `generation_idx`: 0-7 (8 generations per document)
   - `prompt`: the input prompt
   - `output`: parsed JSON output (list of triples)
   - `raw_text`: the raw generation text
   - `format_ok`: boolean
   - `truncated`: boolean
   - `gold_triples`: list of gold triples with `head`, `relation`, `tail`, `evidence`, `hard_negative_sent_ids`
   - `sents`: list of document sentences

2. **CED scoring logic** from `ced_reward.py`:
   - `verbalize_triple(head, relation, tail)` → claim string
   - For each triple: `p_pos = NLI_entail(concat(cited_evidence), claim)`, `p_neg = max(NLI_entail(neg_sent_j, claim))`, `R_CED = max(0, p_pos - p_neg) * I(p_pos > tau)`
   - Uses `cross-encoder/nli-deberta-v3-base` model

3. **Progress**: I'm understanding the full data pipeline and how the CED reward mechanism evaluates triples based on entailment scores between evidence and claims.

Now I need to build a diagnostic script that loads all generations from the JSONL file, groups them by document ID, and for each set of 8 generations per document, I'll parse the extracted triples, compute their CED scores using the NLI model, and measure evidence overlap with Jaccard similarity to produce summary statistics.

The tricky part is understanding what "CED score for a generation" actually means—the existing scoring logic in `rsft_score_filter.py` matches predicted triples against gold triples and uses the gold's evidence and hard negatives to compute the reward, so I need to figure out how to adapt that for evaluating arbitrary generations. For each predicted triple that matches a gold triple, I'm using the predicted evidence sentences as the cited evidence and the gold triple's hard negatives as the negative examples to compute the CED reward. Now I need to figure out the verbalization step—the code uses relation templates to convert triples into natural language claims, but the data has full relation names rather than P-IDs, so I'll need to find or create a fallback verbalization approach. I need to leverage the freige package directly since it's available on the server. I'm setting up a diagnostic script that imports the CED reward model and verbalize_triple function, then loads the NLI model and processes all generations to analyze CED score variance.

Writing the diagnostic script... I'm building a gold triple map for comparison, then iterating through predicted triples to match them against the gold set and compute CED scores based on evidence.

Still writing validation logic... Writing the evaluation pipeline... Completing the statistics collection...

I'm finishing up the document statistics by computing the mean and standard deviation of the CED scores, along with the Jaccard similarity of evidence sets. Then I'm extracting summary statistics across all documents—collecting the standard deviations, means, and Jaccard values—and calculating the gap between the top 2 and bottom 2 scores for documents that have at least 4 generations.

Now I'm printing out the diagnostic results, starting with the total number of documents analyzed and then displaying the variance metrics for CED scores across the dataset. Printing out the distribution metrics...

Now I'm checking the viability based on standard deviation and gap thresholds—if the mean standard deviation is above 0.05 and the gap between top and bottom performers is greater than 0.1, it's viable; below 0.02 standard deviation means it's not viable; otherwise it's marginal. I need to verify that `verbalize_triple` can handle the full relation names like "country" and "headquarters location" rather than property codes, since that's what the data is using.
```

```text
{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"def verbalize_triple(head: str, relation: str, tail: str) -> str:\\n    template = VERBALIZATION_TEMPLATES.get(relation)\\n    if template:\\n        return template.format(head=head, tail=tail)\\n    from freige.data.docred_processor import DOCRED_REL_INFO\\n    rel_name = DOCRED_REL_INFO.get(relation, relation)\\n    return f\\\"{head} {rel_name} {tail}.\\\"\\n\\n\\nclass CEDRewardModel:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"CED 对比证据判别奖励模型。\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n    def __init__(\\n        self,\\n        model_name: str = DEFAULT_NLI_MODEL,\\n        device: Optional[str] = None,\\n        max_length: int = 512,\\n    ):\\n        self.device = device or (\\\"cuda\\\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \\\"cpu\\\")\\n        self.max_length = max_length\\n\\n        logger.info(\\\"Loading NLI model: %s\\\", model_name)\\n        self.tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(model_name)\\n        self.model = AutoModelForSequenceClassification.from_pretrained(model_name)\\n        self.model.to(self.device)\\n        self.model.eval()\\n\\n        self._resolve_entailment_idx()\\n\\n    def _resolve_entailment_idx(self):\\n        id2label = self.model.config.id2label\\n        self.entail_idx = None\\n        for idx, label in id2label.items():\\n            if label.lower() in (\\\"entailment\\\", \\\"entail\\\"):\\n                self.entail_idx = int(idx)\\n                break\\n        if self.entail_idx is None:\\n            logger.warning(\\n                \\\"Could not find 'entailment' in id2label=%s, defaulting to index 1\\\",\\n                id2label,\\n            )\\n            self.entail_idx = 1\\n        logger.info(\\\"Entailment index: %d (id2label=%s)\\\", self.entail_idx, id2label)\\n\\n    @torch.no_grad()\\n    def nli_entailment_prob(\\n        self, premises: list[str], hypotheses: list[str]\\n    ) -> torch.Tensor:\\n        inputs = self.tokenizer(\\n            premises,\\n            hypotheses,\\n            padding=True,\\n            truncation=True,\\n            max_length=self.max_length,\\n            return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\",\\n        ).to(self.device)\\n\\n        logits = self.model(**inputs).logits\\n        probs = F.softmax(logits, dim=-1)\\n        return probs[:, self.entail_idx].cpu()\\n\\n    def compute_ced_reward(\\n        self,\\n        claim: str,\\n        cited_evidence_sents: list[str],\\n        hard_negative_sents: list[str],\\n        tau: float = 0.5,\\n    ) -> dict:\\n        evidence_concat = \\\" \\\".join(cited_evidence_sents)\\n        p_pos = self.nli_entailment_prob(\\n            [evidence_concat], [claim]\\n        ).item()\\n\\n        if not hard_negative_sents:\\n            p_neg = 0.0\\n        else:\\n            neg_probs = self.nli_entailment_prob(\\n                hard_negative_sents, [claim] * len(hard_negative_sents)\\n            )\\n            p_neg = neg_probs.max().item()\\n\\n        margin = p_pos - p_neg\\n        reward = max(0.0, margin) * float(p_pos > tau)\\n\\n        return {\\n            \\\"reward\\\": reward,\\n            \\\"p_pos\\\": p_pos,\\n            \\\"p_neg\\\": p_neg,\\n            \\\"margin\\\": margin,\\n        }\\n\\n    def compute_ced_reward_batch(\\n        self,\\n        claims: list[str],\\n        cited_evidence_list: list[list[str]],\\n        hard_negative_list: list[list[str]],\\n        tau: float = 0.5,\\n    ) -> list[dict]:\\n        n = len(claims)\\n        assert len(cited_evidence_list) == n and len(hard_negative_list) == n\\n\\n        pos_premises = [\\\" \\\".join(ev) for ev in cited_evidence_list]\\n        pos_probs = self.nli_entailment_prob(pos_premises, claims)\\n\\n        all_neg_premises = []\\n        all_neg_hyps = []\\n        neg_counts = []\\n        for i in range(n):\\n            negs = hard_negative_list[i]\\n            neg_counts.append(len(negs))\\n            all_neg_premises.extend(negs)\\n            all_neg_hyps.extend([claims[i]] * len(negs))\\n\\n        if all_neg_premises:\\n            all_neg_probs = self.nli_entailment_prob(all_neg_premises, all_neg_hyps)\\n        else:\\n            all_neg_probs = torch.tensor([])\\n\\n        results = []\\n        offset = 0\\n        for i in range(n):\\n            p_pos = pos_probs[i].item()\\n            nc = neg_counts[i]\\n            if nc > 0:\\n                p_neg = all_neg_probs[offset:offset + nc].max().item()\\n            else:\\n                p_neg = 0.0\\n            offset += nc\\n\\n            margin = p_pos - p_neg\\n            reward = max(0.0, margin) * float(p_pos > tau)\\n            results.append({\\n                \\\"reward\\\": reward,\\n                \\\"p_pos\\\": p_pos,\\n                \\\"p_neg\\\": p_neg,\\n                \\\"margin\\\": margin,\\n            })\\n\\n        return results\\n\\n    def compute_flat_nli_reward(\\n        self,\\n        claim: str,\\n        cited_evidence_sents: list[str],\\n        tau: float = 0.5,\\n    ) -> dict:\\n        evidence_concat = \\\" \\\".join(cited_evidence_sents)\\n        p_pos = self.nli_entailment_prob(\\n            [evidence_concat], [claim]\\n        ).item()\\n        reward = p_pos * float(p_pos > tau)\\n        return {\\\"reward\\\": reward, \\\"p_pos\\\": p_pos}\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.31}}"}
```

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Now I have the complete picture. The `verbalize_triple` function:
1. Tries VERBALIZATION_TEMPLATES with relation as key (P-code based)
2. Falls back to `DOCRED_REL_INFO` lookup
3. Final fallback: `"{head} {rel_name} {tail}."`

Since the data uses full relation names (e.g., "country", "headquarters location"), it'll likely hit the fallback and produce `"{head} country {tail}."` etc. That's fine.

Now, there's a subtlety: in the CED computation for RSFT scoring, the function `compute_ced_reward_for_record` in rsft_score_filter.py uses the **predicted** evidence (from the generation) and the **gold** hard_negative_sent_ids. 

For each predicted triple matching a gold triple, I compute the claim by verbalizing it, then gather the cited evidence from the predicted evidence IDs and the hard negatives from the gold triple's hard_negative_sent_ids — this measures how well the predicted evidence supports the claim against the hard negatives.

Now I'm writing a diagnostic script to process all 1744 records efficiently. For each generation, I'll parse the output and compute CED for every predicted triple that matches a gold triple.

Writing the diagnostic script...
```

## 诊断结果

**VERDICT: VIABLE** — RSFT 可行，继续跑生成。

| 指标 | 值 | 判断阈值 |
|------|------|---------|
| mean_std(ced_score) | **0.1189** | >0.05 ✓ |
| median_std(ced_score) | 0.0992 | — |
| top2-bottom2 gap | **0.2736** | >0.1 ✓ |
| median top2-bot2 gap | 0.2364 | — |
| mean evidence Jaccard | 0.8680 | (高=evidence重叠多) |

**CED 分布**: min=0.00, p25=0.35, median=0.57, p75=0.78, max=1.00

**关键发现**：

- 虽然 triple-set diversity 很低（Jaccard 0.87 = evidence 句子高度重叠），但 **CED score 方差足够大**（std=0.12, top2-bot2 gap=0.27）
- 这意味着即使 8 个 generation 提取的 triple 集合相似，它们选择的 evidence 质量有明显差异
- RSFT 用 CED score 做 top-k 筛选是有效的——每个 doc 的 best 和 worst generation 平均 CED 差 0.27

**结论**：diversity 危机只存在于 triple-set 层面，CED score 维度仍有充足选择信号。继续跑 RSFT 生成，后续用 top-25% CED 筛选即可。

（236 docs 已生成，详细 per-doc 结果存在 `/root/autodl-tmp/rsft_diversity_diag_results.json`）
